Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the tokenization space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 105 and the convergence rate is O(614/n^4).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_105(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 105. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^614(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 221.5 (Spectral Property)

Statement: Under the spectral property assumption on tokenization structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 853. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 890 with relations in degree 5.
